AMPHORA — GENERAL COLLECTION · 500 PROMPTS · BIBLE v3 (renaissance figures, physical shards, urban variants)
Settings: 3:4 or 4:5 portrait · FULL model (not flash) · highest resolution
============================================================================

### GC-001 · Fragment 001 — Fortune
A fragment of a vast painted chapel ceiling, from the zone of Fortune: the goddess Fortuna, blindfolded, glowing in candle-warm light rising from below, pouring coins and small treasures from a cornucopia toward the viewer, crimson drapery streaming, winged figures tumbling after the falling gold. Photographed as a real detached fresco fragment: a physical piece of painted plaster with visible thickness and rough mortar at its broken edges, resting on a plain warm-white studio ground with a soft subtle shadow beneath it. Its upper edge carries a surviving arc of gilded ceiling moulding; the rest is broken plaster. The fragment is large in frame, extending close to all four edges. Within the painted surface, the image is executed in the manner of an Italian Baroque ceiling fresco by an old master: mature, classically proportioned figures with naturalistic adult faces and anatomy, dramatic sotto-in-su foreshortening, wind-blown drapery, sculpted clouds, trompe-l'oeil gilded architecture, warm palette of ochre, venetian red, deep umber, slate blue and burnished gold leaf, fine aged craquelure and plaster losses across the surface. Painterly and hand-made throughout: visible brushwork, true fresco texture. Strictly avoid: childlike or doll-like faces, cartoon proportions, digital smoothness, 3D-render gloss, photographic realism inside the painting. An entirely original composition, not a copy of any existing mural. Maximum detail and fidelity. No text, no signature, no watermark.

### GC-002 · Fragment 002 — Fortune
A fragment of a vast painted chapel ceiling, from the zone of Fortune: a great gilded wheel turned by a winged figure in slate-blue drapery, glowing in candle-warm light rising from below, figures rising on one side and falling from the other. Photographed as a real detached fresco fragment: a physical piece of painted plaster with visible thickness and rough mortar at its broken edges, resting on a plain warm-white studio ground with a soft subtle shadow beneath it. Its silhouette is cracked and chipped with small losses, a remnant of carved gilt cornice surviving along one edge. The fragment is large in frame, extending close to all four edges. Within the painted surface, the image is executed in the manner of an Italian Baroque ceiling fresco by an old master: mature, classically proportioned figures with naturalistic adult faces and anatomy, dramatic sotto-in-su foreshortening, wind-blown drapery, sculpted clouds, trompe-l'oeil gilded architecture, warm palette of ochre, venetian red, deep umber, slate blue and burnished gold leaf, fine aged craquelure and plaster losses across the surface. Painterly and hand-made throughout: visible brushwork, true fresco texture. Strictly avoid: childlike or doll-like faces, cartoon proportions, digital smoothness, 3D-render gloss, photographic realism inside the painting. An entirely original composition, not a copy of any existing mural. Maximum detail and fidelity. No text, no signature, no watermark.

### GC-003 · Fragment 003 — Fortune · URBAN
A fragment of a vast painted chapel ceiling, from the zone of Fortune: a young gambler in wind-blown robes casting painted dice across a cloud-top table, under cool slate light with warmth only in the gilding, an older robed figure watching with folded hands. As a quiet anachronism painted in identical fresco technique: a length of chain-link fence, painted in perfect fresco technique, weaves through the clouds like a garland. Photographed as a real detached fresco fragment: a physical piece of painted plaster with visible thickness and rough mortar at its broken edges, resting on a plain warm-white studio ground with a soft subtle shadow beneath it. It is split into three shards of unequal size, arranged nearly touching, cracks radiating from the breaks. The fragment is large in frame, extending close to all four edges. Within the painted surface, the image is executed in the manner of an Italian Baroque ceiling fresco by an old master: mature, classically proportioned figures with naturalistic adult faces and anatomy, dramatic sotto-in-su foreshortening, wind-blown drapery, sculpted clouds, trompe-l'oeil gilded architecture, warm palette of ochre, venetian red, deep umber, slate blue and burnished gold leaf, fine aged craquelure and plaster losses across the surface. Painterly and hand-made throughout: visible brushwork, true fresco texture. Strictly avoid: childlike or doll-like faces, cartoon proportions, digital smoothness, 3D-render gloss, photographic realism inside the painting. An entirely original composition, not a copy of any existing mural. Maximum detail and fidelity. No text, no signature, no watermark.

### GC-004 · Fragment 004 — Fortune
A fragment of a vast painted chapel ceiling, from the zone of Fortune: the personification of Chance balancing on a rolling golden sphere, lit by amber light from an unseen dawn, her scarf lifting in the wind, coins orbiting her like moons. Photographed as a real detached fresco fragment: a physical piece of painted plaster with visible thickness and rough mortar at its broken edges, resting on a plain warm-white studio ground with a soft subtle shadow beneath it. One side is bitten deep where plaster fell away; a corner of a gilt picture-frame moulding survives on the opposite edge. The fragment is large in frame, extending close to all four edges. Within the painted surface, the image is executed in the manner of an Italian Baroque ceiling fresco by an old master: mature, classically proportioned figures with naturalistic adult faces and anatomy, dramatic sotto-in-su foreshortening, wind-blown drapery, sculpted clouds, trompe-l'oeil gilded architecture, warm palette of ochre, venetian red, deep umber, slate blue and burnished gold leaf, fine aged craquelure and plaster losses across the surface. An entirely original composition, not a copy of any existing mural. Maximum detail and fidelity. No text, no signature, no watermark.

### GC-005 · Fragment 005 — Fortune · URBAN
A fragment of a vast painted chapel ceiling, from the zone of Fortune: two winged figures quarrelling over a single gold coin on a cloud bank, beneath a shaft of pale light falling from a painted opening in the sky, a third laughing behind them with an entire spilled purse. ### GC-009 · Fragment 009 — Fortune

Its upper edge carries a surviving arc of gilded ceiling moulding; the rest is broken plaster. The fragment is large in frame, extending close to all four edges. Within the painted surface, the image is executed in the manner of an Italian Baroque ceiling fresco by an old master: mature, classically proportioned figures with naturalistic adult faces and anatomy, dramatic sotto-in-su foreshortening, wind-blown drapery, sculpted clouds, trompe-l'oeil gilded architecture, warm palette of ochre, venetian red, deep umber, slate blue and burnished gold leaf, fine aged craquelure and plaster losses across the surface. Painterly and hand-made throughout: visible brushwork, true fresco texture. Strictly avoid: childlike or doll-like faces, cartoon proportions, digital smoothness, 3D-render gloss, photographic realism inside the painting. An entirely original composition, not a copy of any existing mural. Maximum detail and fidelity. No text, no signature, no watermark.

Within the painted surface, the image is executed in the manner of an Italian Baroque ceiling fresco by an old master: mature, classically proportioned figures with naturalistic adult faces and anatomy, dramatic sotto-in-su foreshortening, wind-blown drapery, sculpted clouds, trompe-l'oeil gilded architecture, warm palette of ochre, venetian red, deep umber, slate blue and burnished gold leaf, fine aged craquelure and plaster losses across the surface. Painterly and hand-made throughout: visible brushwork, true fresco texture. Strictly avoid: childlike or doll-like faces, cartoon proportions, digital smoothness, 3D-render gloss, photographic realism inside the painting. An entirely original composition, not a copy of any existing mural. Maximum detail and fidelity. No text, no signature, no watermark.

Within the painted surface, the image is executed in the manner of an Italian Baroque ceiling fresco by an old master: mature, classically proportioned figures with naturalistic adult faces and anatomy, dramatic sotto-in-su foreshortening, wind-blown drapery, sculpted clouds, trompe-l'oeil gilded architecture, warm palette of ochre, venetian red, deep umber, slate blue and burnished gold leaf, fine aged craquelure and plaster losses across the surface. Painterly and hand-made throughout: visible brushwork, true fresco texture. Strictly avoid: childlike or doll-like faces, cartoon proportions, digital smoothness, 3D-render gloss, photographic realism inside the painting. An entirely original composition, not a copy of any existing mural. Maximum detail and fidelity. No text, no signature, no watermark.

Within the painted surface, the image is executed in the manner of an Italian Baroque ceiling fresco by an old master: mature, classically proportioned figures with naturalistic adult faces and anatomy, dramatic sotto-in-su foreshortening, wind-blown drapery, sculpted clouds, trompe-l'oeil gilded architecture, warm palette of ochre, venetian red, deep umber, slate blue and burnished gold leaf, fine aged craquelure and plaster losses across the surface. Painterly and hand-made throughout: visible brushwork, true fresco texture. Strictly avoid: childlike or doll-like faces, cartoon proportions, digital smoothness, 3D-render gloss, photographic realism inside the painting. An entirely original composition, not a copy of any existing mural. Maximum detail and fidelity. No text, no signature, no watermark.

Within the painted surface, the image is executed in the manner of an Italian Baroque ceiling fresco by an old master: mature, classically proportioned figures with naturalistic adult faces and anatomy, dramatic sotto-in-su foreshortening, wind-blown drapery, sculpted clouds, trompe-l'oeil gilded architecture, warm palette of ochre, venetian red, deep umber, slate blue and burnished gold leaf, fine aged craquelure and plaster losses across the surface. Painterly and hand-made throughout: visible brushwork, true fresco texture. Strictly avoid: childlike or doll-like faces, cartoon proportions, digital smoothness, 3D-render gloss, photographic realism inside the painting. An entirely original composition, not a copy of any existing mural. Maximum detail and fidelity. No text, no signature, no watermark.

A fragment of a vast painted chapel ceiling, from the zone of the Trades: a shipwright fitting a crescent moon as the keel of a celestial boat, caught in a burst of golden light breaking through painted clouds. As a quiet anachronism painted in identical fresco technique: a length of chain-link fence, painted in perfect fresco technique, weaves through the clouds like a garland. Photographed as a real detached fresco fragment: a physical piece of painted plaster with visible thickness and rough mortar at its broken edges, resting on a plain warm-white studio ground with a soft subtle shadow beneath it. One side is bitten deep where plaster fell away; a corner of a gilt picture-frame moulding survives on the opposite edge. The fragment is large in frame, extending close to all four edges. Within the painted surface, the image is executed in the manner of an Italian Baroque ceiling fresco by an old master: mature, classically proportioned figures with naturalistic adult faces and anatomy, dramatic sotto-in-su foreshortening, wind-blown drapery, sculpted clouds, trompe-l'oeil gilded architecture, warm palette of ochre, venetian red, deep umber, slate blue and burnished gold leaf, fine aged craquelure and plaster losses across the surface. Painterly and hand-made throughout: visible brushwork, true fresco texture. Strictly avoid: childlike or doll-like faces, cartoon proportions, digital smoothness, 3D-render gloss, photographic realism inside the painting. An entirely original composition, not a copy of any existing mural. Maximum detail and fidelity. No text, no signature, no watermark.

Painterly and hand-made throughout: visible brushwork, true fresco texture. Strictly avoid: childlike or doll-like faces, cartoon proportions, digital smoothness, 3D-render gloss, photographic realism inside the painting. Within the painted surface, the image is executed in the manner of an Italian Baroque ceiling fresco by an old master: mature, classically proportioned figures with naturalistic adult faces and anatomy, dramatic sotto-in-su foreshortening, wind-blown drapery, sculpted clouds, trompe-l'oeil gilded architecture, warm palette of ochre, venetian red, deep umber, slate blue and burnished gold leaf, fine aged craquelure and plaster losses across the surface. Painterly and hand-made throughout: visible brushwork, true fresco texture. Strictly avoid: childlike or doll-like faces, cartoon proportions, digital smoothness, 3D-render gloss, photographic realism inside the painting. An entirely original composition, not a copy of any existing mural. Maximum detail and fidelity. No text, no signature, no watermark.

### GC-201 · Fragment 201 — The Vices
A fragment of a vast painted chapel ceiling, from the zone of the Vices: Greed as a richly robed figure clutching a chest of gold that is dissolving into smoke between his fingers, glowing in candle-warm light rising from below. Photographed as a real detached fresco fragment: a physical piece of painted plaster with visible thickness and rough mortar at its broken edges, resting on a plain warm-white studio ground with a soft subtle shadow beneath it. One side is bitten deep where plaster fell away; a corner of a gilt picture-frame moulding survives on the opposite edge. The fragment is large in frame, extending close to all four edges. Within the painted surface, the image is executed in the manner of an Italian Baroque ceiling fresco by an old master: mature, classically proportioned figures with naturalistic adult faces and anatomy, dramatic sotto-in-su foreshortening, wind-blown drapery, sculpted clouds, trompe-l'oeil gilded architecture, warm palette of ochre, venetian red, deep umber, slate blue and burnished gold leaf, fine aged craquelure and plaster losses across the surface. Painterly and hand-made throughout: visible brushwork, true fresco texture. Strictly avoid: childlike or doll-like faces, cartoon proportions, digital smoothness, 3D-render gloss, photographic realism inside the painting. An entirely original composition, not a copy of any existing mural. Maximum detail and fidelity. No text, no signature, no watermark.

Within the painted surface, the image is executed in the manner of an Italian Baroque ceiling fresco by an old master: mature, classically proportioned figures with naturalistic adult faces and anatomy, dramatic sotto-in-su foreshortening, wind-blown drapery, sculpted clouds, trompe-l'oeil gilded architecture, warm palette of ochre, venetian red, deep umber, slate blue and burnished gold leaf, fine aged craquelure and plaster losses across the surface. Painterly and hand-made throughout: visible brushwork, true fresco texture. Strictly avoid: childlike or doll-like faces, cartoon proportions, digital smoothness, 3D-render gloss, photographic realism inside the painting. An entirely original composition, not a copy of any existing mural. Maximum detail and fidelity. No text, no signature, no watermark.

Strictly avoid: childlike or doll-like faces, cartoon proportions, digital smoothness, 3D-render gloss, photographic realism inside the painting. An entirely original composition, not a copy of any existing mural. Maximum detail and fidelity. No text, no signature, no watermark.

### GC-246 · Fragment 246 — The Vices
A fragment of a vast painted chapel ceiling, from the zone of the Vices: the Vices in chains being led in procession by a small robed figure bearing a single candle, under cool slate light with warmth only in the gilding. Photographed as a real detached fresco fragment: a physical piece of painted plaster with visible thickness and rough mortar at its broken edges, resting on a plain warm-white studio ground with a soft subtle shadow beneath it. It is split into three shards of unequal size, arranged nearly touching, cracks radiating from the breaks. The fragment is large in frame, extending close to all four edges. Within the painted surface, the image is executed in the manner of an Italian Baroque ceiling fresco by an old master: mature, classically proportioned figures with naturalistic adult faces and anatomy, dramatic sotto-in-su foreshortening, wind-blown drapery, sculpted clouds, trompe-l'oeil gilded architecture, warm palette of ochre, venetian red, deep umber, slate blue and burnished gold leaf, fine aged craquelure and plaster losses across the surface. Within the painted surface, the image is executed in the manner of an Italian Baroque ceiling fresco by an old master: mature, classically proportioned figures with naturalistic adult faces and anatomy, dramatic sotto-in-su foreshortening, wind-blown drapery, sculpted clouds, trompe-l'oeil gilded architecture, warm palette of ochre, venetian red, deep umber, slate blue and burnished gold leaf, fine aged craquelure and plaster losses across the surface. Painterly and hand-made throughout: visible brushwork, true fresco texture. Strictly avoid: childlike or doll-like faces, cartoon proportions, digital smoothness, 3D-render gloss, photographic realism inside the painting. An entirely original composition, not a copy of any existing mural. Maximum detail and fidelity. No text, no signature, no watermark.

### GC-291 · Fragment 291 — The Seasons
A fragment of a vast painted chapel ceiling, from the zone of the Seasons: Autumn as a figure shaking a great vine so grapes fall to gathered figures below the cloud line, beneath a shaft of pale light falling from a painted opening in the sky. Photographed as a real detached fresco fragment: a physical piece of painted plaster with visible thickness and rough mortar at its broken edges, resting on a plain warm-white studio ground with a soft subtle shadow beneath it. Its silhouette is cracked and chipped with small losses, a remnant of carved gilt cornice surviving along one edge. The fragment is large in frame, extending close to all four edges. Within the painted surface, the image is executed in the manner of an Italian Baroque ceiling fresco by an old master: mature, classically proportioned figures with naturalistic adult faces and anatomy, dramatic sotto-in-su foreshortening, wind-blown drapery, sculpted clouds, trompe-l'oeil gilded architecture, warm palette of ochre, venetian red, deep umber, slate blue and burnished gold leaf, fine aged craquelure and plaster losses across the surface. Painterly and hand-made throughout: visible brushwork, true fresco texture. Strictly avoid: childlike or doll-like faces, cartoon proportions, digital smoothness, 3D-render gloss, photographic realism inside the painting. Within the painted surface, the image is executed in the manner of an Italian Baroque ceiling fresco by an old master: mature, classically proportioned figures with naturalistic adult faces and anatomy, dramatic sotto-in-su foreshortening, wind-blown drapery, sculpted clouds, trompe-l'oeil gilded architecture, warm palette of ochre, venetian red, deep umber, slate blue and burnished gold leaf, fine aged craquelure and plaster losses across the surface. Painterly and hand-made throughout: visible brushwork, true fresco texture. Strictly avoid: childlike or doll-like faces, cartoon proportions, digital smoothness, 3D-render gloss, photographic realism inside the painting. An entirely original composition, not a copy of any existing mural. Maximum detail and fidelity. No text, no signature, no watermark.

As a quiet anachronism painted in identical fresco technique: a length of chain-link fence, painted in perfect fresco technique, weaves through the clouds like a garland. Photographed as a real detached fresco fragment: a physical piece of painted plaster with visible thickness and rough mortar at its broken edges, resting on a plain warm-white studio ground with a soft subtle shadow beneath it. Within the painted surface, the image is executed in the manner of an Italian Baroque ceiling fresco by an old master: mature, classically proportioned figures with naturalistic adult faces and anatomy, dramatic sotto-in-su foreshortening, wind-blown drapery, sculpted clouds, trompe-l'oeil gilded architecture, warm palette of ochre, venetian red, deep umber, slate blue and burnished gold leaf, fine aged craquelure and plaster losses across the surface. Painterly and hand-made throughout: visible brushwork, true fresco texture. Strictly avoid: childlike or doll-like faces, cartoon proportions, digital smoothness, 3D-render gloss, photographic realism inside the painting. An entirely original composition, not a copy of any existing mural. Maximum detail and fidelity. No text, no signature, no watermark.

### GC-381 · Fragment 381 — The Muses · URBAN
A fragment of a vast painted chapel ceiling, from the zone of the Muses: the muse of Tragedy holding a mask that weeps real painted tears into a gilded cup, under cool slate light with warmth only in the gilding. As a quiet anachronism painted in identical fresco technique: a painted pigeon wearing a tiny gold crown perches on the trompe-l'oeil cornice. Photographed as a real detached fresco fragment: a physical piece of painted plaster with visible thickness and rough mortar at its broken edges, resting on a plain warm-white studio ground with a soft subtle shadow beneath it. It is split into three shards of unequal size, arranged nearly touching, cracks radiating from the breaks. The fragment is large in frame, extending close to all four edges. Within the painted surface, the image is executed in the manner of an Italian Baroque ceiling fresco by an old master: mature, classically proportioned figures with naturalistic adult faces and anatomy, dramatic sotto-in-su foreshortening, wind-blown drapery, sculpted clouds, trompe-l'oeil gilded architecture, warm palette of ochre, venetian red, deep umber, slate blue and burnished gold leaf, fine aged craquelure and plaster losses across the surface. Painterly and hand-made throughout: visible brushwork, true fresco texture. Within the painted surface, the image is executed in the manner of an Italian Baroque ceiling fresco by an old master: mature, classically proportioned figures with naturalistic adult faces and anatomy, dramatic sotto-in-su foreshortening, wind-blown drapery, sculpted clouds, trompe-l'oeil gilded architecture, warm palette of ochre, venetian red, deep umber, slate blue and burnished gold leaf, fine aged craquelure and plaster losses across the surface. Painterly and hand-made throughout: visible brushwork, true fresco texture. Strictly avoid: childlike or doll-like faces, cartoon proportions, digital smoothness, 3D-render gloss, photographic realism inside the painting. An entirely original composition, not a copy of any existing mural. Maximum detail and fidelity. No text, no signature, no watermark.

Photographed as a real detached fresco fragment: a physical piece of painted plaster with visible thickness and rough mortar at its broken edges, resting on a plain warm-white studio ground with a soft subtle shadow beneath it. Its silhouette is cracked and chipped with small losses, a remnant of carved gilt cornice surviving along one edge. The fragment is large in frame, extending close to all four edges. Within the painted surface, the image is executed in the manner of an Italian Baroque ceiling fresco by an old master: mature, classically proportioned figures with naturalistic adult faces and anatomy, dramatic sotto-in-su foreshortening, wind-blown drapery, sculpted clouds, trompe-l'oeil gilded architecture, warm palette of ochre, venetian red, deep umber, slate blue and burnished gold leaf, fine aged craquelure and plaster losses across the surface. Painterly and hand-made throughout: visible brushwork, true fresco texture. Strictly avoid: childlike or doll-like faces, cartoon proportions, digital smoothness, 3D-render gloss, photographic realism inside the painting. An entirely original composition, not a copy of any existing mural. Maximum detail and fidelity. No text, no signature, no watermark.

Within the painted surface, the image is executed in the manner of an Italian Baroque ceiling fresco by an old master: mature, classically proportioned figures with naturalistic adult faces and anatomy, dramatic sotto-in-su foreshortening, wind-blown drapery, sculpted clouds, trompe-l'oeil gilded architecture, warm palette of ochre, venetian red, deep umber, slate blue and burnished gold leaf, fine aged craquelure and plaster losses across the surface. Painterly and hand-made throughout: visible brushwork, true fresco texture. Strictly avoid: childlike or doll-like faces, cartoon proportions, digital smoothness, 3D-render gloss, photographic realism inside the painting. A fragment of a vast painted chapel ceiling, from the zone of the Feast: figures beneath the feast table in the clouds, sharing stolen sweetmeats with a dog of white mist, under cool slate light with warmth only in the gilding. Photographed as a real detached fresco fragment: a physical piece of painted plaster with visible thickness and rough mortar at its broken edges, resting on a plain warm-white studio ground with a soft subtle shadow beneath it. It is split into three shards of unequal size, arranged nearly touching, cracks radiating from the breaks. The fragment is large in frame, extending close to all four edges. Within the painted surface, the image is executed in the manner of an Italian Baroque ceiling fresco by an old master: mature, classically proportioned figures with naturalistic adult faces and anatomy, dramatic sotto-in-su foreshortening, wind-blown drapery, sculpted clouds, trompe-l'oeil gilded architecture, warm palette of ochre, venetian red, deep umber, slate blue and burnished gold leaf, fine aged craquelure and plaster losses across the surface. Painterly and hand-made throughout: visible brushwork, true fresco texture. Strictly avoid: childlike or doll-like faces, cartoon proportions, digital smoothness, 3D-render gloss, photographic realism inside the painting. An entirely original composition, not a copy of any existing mural. Maximum detail and fidelity. No text, no signature, no watermark.
